- 독립 검증기는 raw code 0..219를 실행해 compact 목표 13/13과 stock tail 207/207을 확인했다. UV helper는 synthetic 960..972와 경계값을 실행했고 U/V 13/13, stock 복귀, J-only 훅, delay slot, helper 내부 load 없음과 RA 불변을 통과했다. 원본 compact plane 재구성, 중앙 6열 샘플, 설정문 3개와 E7 배열 2개도 모두 PASS다.
- `V326.bin` SHA-256 `78A7B7AD30A5201FEFA7A8CA1BECE1ADF8619658E402DE48D523A4973DA7FAC2`, CUE `5AE10F15EF5165B38085813F114B223E5396F8108BBA7F39968A30E7EA2C59BE`. 데이터 LBA 506/506, COMM 307/667/891, PSX.EXE LBA 268481, 164/164 패치 멤버 readback PASS다.
- 정적 helper 진리표는 U/V만 확인해, helper 진입 전 `0x8016B524`에서 설정되고 복귀 후 `0x8016B640`에서 소비되는 live `$t1=160`을 검사하지 못했다. 또한 raw helper가 공용 decoder 앞단에서 상태 폭 없이 한글 코드를 synthetic으로 바꾸는 결함도 놓쳤다. V326은 폐기했고 두 결함은 각각 V327/V329에서 복구했다.

2026-08-26 | V325 Hanme16 UI 포인터 전수 재인코딩 | py_compile, 2회 재빌드, 독립 문자/비트맵/Expected-Write 검증, V324 check_build 대조, mkpsxiso/LBA | STATIC UI/LAYOUT PASS, INHERITED DAT DEBT FAIL, RUNTIME PENDING.
- full/delta SHA-256은 `7828AA04F6A0684981332924C30B4139ABFCA5065138FA899C4D429E87C74CD1` / `3A531423DBA076557BA67548E0161813CBA8CB01D253DD70C79F753DC2E3EE7B`로 2회 동일했다. 변경 멤버는 `PSX.EXE`, `COMM.IMG` 두 개, 변경 바이트는 7,284/226개다.
- 독립 검증기는 666포인터/561고유 payload를 디스크에서 다시 읽고 각 문자 경계의 token을 physical `<960`으로 해석했다. 한글은 공식 Hanme 8x4x4 조합 비트맵, ASCII는 `ascii_16px.pkl`과 전수 일치했다. 공백은 `A1/physical160` 697회이고 문자 경계의 literal `9C`는 0회다. COMM 변경 plane은 `403,762,819,823,865` 정확히 5개다.
- UI 풀은 5,590/6,076바이트, free 486바이트이며 미사용 바이트는 전부 0이다. 모든 DAT 162개, V324 resident lookup/기술 범위 커서/HUD binary 포인터는 byte exact다.
- `check_build.py`의 V325 결과는 선택지 폭 75, 선택지 마커 2, 4줄 초과 18건이다. 세 집합은 V324와 동일한 상속 부채이며 V325 신규 DAT 회귀가 아니다. V324에 있던 EXE 포인터 중간 착지 1건은 V325 UI 풀 재배치 후 사라졌다.
- `V325.bin` SHA-256 `3A3DE2389560B79202749FA789C65F20E5E56F3511043315D0A9C5A273E40493`, CUE `5EBDC8CEF59FA42C1BEB5C330EBC262F3D3453FC7B2315F940C7D6874E0C1670`. 506/506 데이터 LBA, COMM 307/667/891, PSX.EXE LBA 268481, 패치 멤버 164/164가 PASS했다.
- 실기 확인 항목: 콜드부팅 후 불러오기, Yes/No의 `)` 제거와 반각 공백, 장비·액세서리 이름/설명, 아이템 이름/설명/사용 확인/획득 배너, 기술 이름/설명/전투 프롬프트, 단일·십자·확장 기술 범위 커서. 런타임 승인 전에는 배포하지 않는다.

조치: 낮은 슬롯을 완전히 배제한 안전판 story_bulk3_s1072_room_highslot_patch_only.zip 생성.
기준: 03_output/story_progress_bulk3_all_1_gamefiles_only 와 동일한 파일 세트/해시. 즉 벌크3 안전판으로 되돌리는 패치이며, 19-25 및 plus 계열을 기준으로 하지 않음.

2026-08-05 | vertical glyph corruption, user report on six v129 save states | Anchored RAM and VRAM on the strips, confirmed all six states are v129 by the reserved block, extracted the mangled glyph's 12x12 ink mask from the framebuffer and searched the whole VRAM for its source | FAIL, cause found. Any glyph on font rows 22..39 that is not a strip therefore samples page y=0 at the same U/V and draws twelve rows of some other cell.

2026-08-05 | E2 slot terminator, whole-archive scan | Walked every non-empty external slot in every DAT of v129 and looked for a 0x00 in bytes 0..126 | FAIL. Seven slots have none: `21/S2021.DAT` slot 6, `5/S5031.DAT` slots 12, 13, 14 and 15, and `6/S6041.DAT` slots 0 and 1. `SLOT_TEXT_MAX` is 127, so a 127-byte payload fills bytes 0..126 and the builder then writes the `capacity - 2` metadata over byte 127, leaving the string with no terminator at all. The renderer reads on into the next slot. The correct limit is 126: text 0..125, terminator at 126, metadata at 127. This is the freeze the user hit in save state 6.

2026-08-06 | correction: the row budget was measured wrong | The user challenged a warning on `1/S1023.DAT 0x47C3C`, which renders correctly in game. Re-measured the wrap off the framebuffers instead of assuming | The 21-cell model was wrong twice. A space (0x9C) advances 6 pixels, not 12, so counting every token as one cell over-counts any line with spaces. And the window is not the original's row count: it is at least three rows.
- Width, measured: a row holds 228 pixels. In `21/S2041.DAT` the wrap breaks after 진행되 at exactly 228 and the next glyph would reach 240. In `1/S1061.DAT` the second row reaches 228 and the following space and glyph would reach 294. So the row is 228..239 pixels and 228 is the safe figure -- nineteen full-width glyphs.
- Height: `max(original rows, 3)`. The user's line has a two-row original, needs three rows of Korean and works. The ship line has a three-row original, needs four, and freezes. A floor of 4 would flag nothing at all and cannot be right, since the ship line does freeze.

2026-08-06 | the encoder could only see half the code space | The user photographed 말괄량이 덕분에 rendering correctly in game while the editor called 괄, 량 and 덕 impossible. Decoded that line out of the built archive | The codes are 0x3C, 0x40 and 0x44 -- one-byte codes. `build_encoder` enumerated the two-byte range and the lookup table but hardcoded the one-byte range to 26 ASCII entries, so 194 of the 220 one-byte codes were never looked at. 118 of them hold Korean.
- The mapping is `index = code - 1`, and the two-byte range continues from exactly where it ends: `DD 01` is index 220 and the one-byte range stops at 219. Confirmed twice -- against the original script's own decoded text it agrees on all 18 ASCII codes that appear there with no disagreement, and 괄, 량 and 덕 read back as their gulim renders at 59, 63 and 67.
- Latin capitals were invisible for a different reason: the rendered-glyph table names only Hangul. They are now found by taking each letter's cell off the ORIGINAL disc and looking for that picture in the build, which is evidence rather than a name. H at DD FA, L at DD F7, M at DE 4F, P at DD 90. R does not resolve and should not: its original cell at index 732 was overwritten with Korean and the picture exists nowhere in the build.
- Full sweep of every reachable index -- 3,427 of them, one-byte plus two-byte plus the 508 lookup entries -- gives 721 distinct nameable characters. 씩 뜩 맺 밝 엮 맑 are absent from all of them, including the 67 cells in Korean territory the gulim table cannot name, which were read one by one.
- Lines refused for a missing character fall from 74 to 36: 9 uses of R, 16 uses of the six absent syllables, and the rest control markers, embedded newlines and smart quotes.

2026-08-06 | the UI string pool is not corrupted -- my measurement was | Claimed 185 UI strings had overrun their slots. - 세 상태 모두 active_mask=0xFFFFFFFF(28슬롯인데 32비트 전부)로 마스크 오염이 있다. 활성 패킷의 V는 224로 치환돼 있어 selector·frame 표식 치환 경로 자체는 동작했다.
- 정적 검증(업로드 픽셀 28/28, BIOS 회귀, 월드맵 회귀)을 전부 통과한 빌드의 실기 실패이므로, 원인은 정적 재생이 모형화하지 못하는 영역(인터럽트 타이밍, GPU 비동기, CPU-GPU 경쟁)에 있다. 원인 미확정.
- borrow/restore 계열은 v218 부팅 정지, v219b 전면 손상, v220 빈칸으로 3연속 실기 실패다. 이 구조의 후속 후보는 실기 검증 전에 타이밍 모형 검증 방법을 먼저 마련해야 한다.
